Task ID: 0
Agent: main (Full Stack Agent)
Task: Analyze and permanently store `FULLSTACK_AGENT_GUIDE (2).md` as the primary reference for the upcoming project.

Work Log:
- Read the full guide located at `/home/z/my-project/upload/FULLSTACK_AGENT_GUIDE (2).md` (2081 lines, ~85KB).
- Completed a full section-by-section analysis (all 11 parts + 4 appendices).
- Stored this distilled analysis below as the mandatory engineering standard for ALL future tasks in this project.

Stage Summary — MASTER REFERENCE DIGEST (binding rules for every future task):

## A. Target Stack (from guide §1.1)
- Next.js 16 (App Router) · React 19 · TypeScript 5 strict · Tailwind CSS 4 + shadcn/ui
- Prisma 6 + SQLite (dev) · next-intl 4 (AR/EN, RTL/LTR) · NextAuth v4 or custom HMAC sessions
- Zustand (client state) + TanStack Query (server state)
- Heavy libs (Three.js, Chart.js, >50KB): `dynamic(() => import(...), { ssr: false })` with non-null loading fallback

## B. Next.js 16 Critical Rules (§1.3 — these BREAK code if ignored)
1. `params` / `searchParams` are Promises → `const { slug } = await params`
2. `proxy.ts` replaces `middleware.ts` → NEVER create `src/middleware.ts`
3. `cookies()` / `headers()` are async → `const store = await cookies()`
4. `store.set()` only in Server Actions / Route Handlers, never during render
5. `global-error.tsx` ONLY at `src/app/global-error.tsx` (root), must render own `<html>/<body>`
6. Route groups `(name)` don't affect URL → only ONE page.tsx per URL path (no conflicts)
7. `not-found.tsx` is a server component → use `getTranslations()`, NOT `useTranslations()`

## C. Architecture (§1.2, §1.5–1.8)
- Structure: `src/app/[locale]/` (layout, page, loading, error, not-found), `components/{ui,layout,providers,feature}`, `lib/{db,auth,brand,domain}.ts`, `i18n/`, `messages/{en,ar}.json`, `prisma/`
- `'use client'` ONLY for hooks/events/browser APIs/router hooks. NEVER import `@/lib/db` or `fs` into client components
- Hydration safety: gate `Date.now()`, `Math.random()`, `localStorage`, timezone formatting behind `mounted` flag + `useEffect`
- Sticky footer: `min-h-screen flex flex-col` on body + `flex-1` main + `mt-auto` footer + `pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)]`
- Every async route: `loading.tsx` (role="status" + aria-live + sr-only) + `error.tsx` (no stack traces) + segment `not-found.tsx`

## D. Security (§2 — defense in depth)
1. Two-layer auth: edge check in `proxy.ts` + `requireAuth()` as FIRST statement of every admin server action/route
2. Zod validation on EVERY API input before Prisma
3. No raw SQL with user input; dynamic orderBy/field names validated against allow-lists
4. Webhooks: HMAC (timingSafeEqual — NOTE: `crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual` does NOT exist, use node:crypto) + timestamp skew ≤5min + nonce idempotency in Serializable tx
5. Rate limiting on login/contact/checkout/password reset; IP from trusted proxy header (last in XFF chain)
6. Secrets: .env only, never `NEXT_PUBLIC_` prefixed, ≥32 chars, fail closed at startup
7. IDOR: scope queries by `{ id, userId: session.userId }`; admin scopes by brand from session
8. Cookies: httpOnly + secure(prod) + sameSite:'lax' + `__Host-` prefix; maxAge ≤24h
9. Payment: NEVER trust client price — recompute in Serializable tx with 0.001 tolerance
10. Dev-only endpoints → 404 in production
11. No info leaks: no stack traces, no file paths, no schema names, no exact stock counts
12. Security headers centralized in next.config.ts (nosniff, DENY, referrer-policy, permissions-policy, HSTS, CSP)

## E. Database/Prisma (§3)
- Money: `Decimal` (not Float); if Float forced → `KWD_TOLERANCE = 0.001` everywhere
- Status fields: Prisma enums, not strings; always `createdAt`/`updatedAt`
- Compound `@@index` for every common `where` combo; FKs NOT auto-indexed in SQLite — add explicitly
- Explicit `onDelete`: Cascade (OrderItem→Order), SetNull (Booking→Product, nullable FK), Restrict (protect parents)
- Multi-tenant: `brand` field on every tenant model; every query scoped by it (from session, not URL)
- Stock check + booking create INTERLEAVED in ONE Serializable transaction (prevents overbooking)
- Overlap condition: `A.start < B.endEff AND A.endEff > B.start` with endOfDay extension on BOTH sides (symmetric!), strict `<`
- PENDING bookings TTL 30min (lazy GC) in availability queries — DoS prevention
- Catch P2002 (unique) / P2025 (not found) / P2034 (tx conflict) with friendly messages; P2002 is the real TOCTOU safety net
- Multi-item orders: shared generated `orderId` (`ORD-...`); webhook confirms ALL via `updateMany({ where: { orderId } })`
- Normalize dates to UTC midnight server-side (`YYYY-MM-DD` → `T00:00:00.000Z`)
- SQLite path consistency: ONE db path everywhere; verify single *.db file exists

## F. TypeScript & Code Quality (§4)
- strict + noUncheckedIndexedAccess + noImplicitOverride + exactOptionalPropertyTypes
- No `any`/`as any` → use `unknown` + narrowing; no `!` non-null assertions → explicit checks
- No silent catches; loading state reset in `finally` (never only in try or catch)
- React: stable keys (not index), cleanup effects, no derived state in useState, no window reads in render
- Dynamic i18n keys: `t.has(key) ? t(key) : fallback`
- Component size: <100 ideal, <300 acceptable, >500 split
- Brand colors ONCE in `src/lib/brand-colors.ts` + CSS vars via `data-brand`; grep hex values to catch stale refs
- Brand routing helpers in ONE file (`resolveBrandFromPath`, `getProductsBasePath`, `getCartHref`) — never hardcode `/products` in shared components
- Structured JSON logs server-side with `event` + `correlationId`; log IDs, never PII/secrets

## G. i18n (§5)
- EN/AR perfect key parity (parity diff script in CI)
- camelCase keys ONLY (no snake_case duplicates)
- ICU placeholders; Arabic 6 plural forms (zero/one/two/few/many/other)
- `getTranslations()` in server, `useTranslations()` in client only
- RTL: logical properties only (`ms-`, `me-`, `ps-`, `pe-`, `start-`, `end-`); `<html dir="rtl">`; test every page in RTL

## H. Booking/Cart/Checkout Flow (§6)
Cart(localStorage, brand-scoped keys) → checkout validation (products active, price recomputed from DB) → POST /api/orders (Serializable tx, interleaved check+create, one orderId) → payment view (fetch ALL bookings by orderId) → webhook (HMAC + idempotency + state machine validation) → updateMany all to CONFIRMED → success page.
- Payment state machine: PENDING→[CONFIRMED|CANCELLED|PAYMENT_FAILED], CONFIRMED→[COMPLETED|CANCELLED], CANCELLED/COMPLETED terminal, PAYMENT_FAILED→retry allowed. Validate transitions BEFORE update.
- Cross-brand cart: reject or clear with toast

## I. Accessibility (§7 — WCAG 2.1 AA)
- Semantic HTML, keyboard reachable, `focus-visible:ring-2`, 44px touch targets
- aria-label on icon-only buttons WITH context ("Delete Product X")
- Forms: labels, aria-invalid, aria-describedby, errors in `role="alert"` + `text-destructive` (red, never brand color)
- Loading: role="status" + aria-live="polite" + sr-only; toasts: role="alert"
- Modals: focus trap, role="dialog", aria-modal, ESC
- Contrast: 4.5:1 normal text, 3:1 large/UI (no `text-paper/30`-style low opacity)
- 3D/WebGL: dark gradient fallback (no white page on reduced-motion); dynamic import loading ≠ null
- Success states: move focus to message, don't auto-close too fast
- Client-computed values (totals): guard with mounted flag (no 0.000 flash)

## J. Performance & SEO (§8)
- `next/image` everywhere (alt + width/height or fill+sizes); `next/font` (Inter latin + Cairo arabic, preload:false for rare fonts)
- Tree-shaken icon imports; paginated queries (take/skip); select to limit over-fetch; no N+1 (include/select)
- generateStaticParams + dynamicParams on dynamic routes; ISR revalidate where sensible
- Centralized `buildMetadata` with `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` (never hardcode URLs)
- `src/app/sitemap.ts` (all 200-OK URLs only, hreflang alternates, ALL brands) and `src/app/robots.ts` (delete `public/robots.txt` to avoid shadowing)
- JSON-LD (Organization/WebSite) on HOME page ONLY; Product+BreadcrumbList on product pages

## K. Common Pitfalls Catalog (§9 — symptom → fix quick map)
- Next.js 16: params undefined→await Promise; same-path conflict→one page per URL; global-error at root; no middleware.ts
- Security: missing requireAuth; webhook idempotency; PENDING TTL; server-side price recompute; generic stock messages; NODE_ENV gate; P2002 catch; cookie flags
- DB: compound indexes; interleaved Serializable tx; symmetric +24h overlap; onDelete SetNull; single db file path; Decimal for money; shared orderId for multi-item
- Quality: no `!`, finally resets, no silent catch, no `as any`, BRAND_COLORS const, brand path helpers, no dead code, t.has() guard
- i18n: parity, one case convention, getTranslations in server, logical properties, 6 Arabic plurals
- a11y: 3D fallback, contextual aria-labels, mounted guard, focus management, destructive color for errors
- SEO: JSON-LD home only, env URLs, no redirects in sitemap, robots.ts not static file, all brands in sitemap, hreflang
- Perf: dynamic ssr:false for heavy libs, no N+1, pagination, select, next/font, next/image
- Dev env: background dev server with keepalive, bun install after wipes, db:push+seed after db loss, pkill next dev on port conflict, rm -rf .next on stale cache

## L. Loops Audit Methodology (§10)
- Group 1 (read-only analysis): 4 parallel agents — Security / Code Quality / Architecture / Data+i18n — cite file:line, severity (CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW/INFO), concrete fix
- Group 2 (fixers): parallel, non-overlapping file ownership, minimal surgical changes, lint+tsc after, worklog entry
- Round 2: re-verify (FIXED/PARTIALLY/NOT FIXED + regression hunt) — loop until all PASS
- Group 3: 6 fresh reviewers (Security, Business Logic, Architecture, TypeScript, Data/i18n, UI/UX a11y) — fresh eyes, loop until all 6 PASS
- Severity: CRITICAL blocks release, HIGH fix before release, MEDIUM next sprint, LOW backlog, INFO note

## M. Definition of Done (§11 + Appendix C) — "It compiles" is NEVER enough
Checklist: lint 0/0 · tsc 0 errors · i18n parity 0 diff · requireAuth everywhere · Zod on inputs · webhook 3-layer defense · rate limits · cookie flags · no NEXT_PUBLIC secrets · NODE_ENV gates · indexes · onDelete · Serializable interleaved tx · PENDING TTL · symmetric overlap · shared orderId · P2002/P2025/P2034 handled · await params · proxy.ts not middleware · global-error at root · one page per URL · generateStaticParams · metadata per page · minimal use client · dynamic heavy libs · no hydration mismatch · loading/error files · sticky footer · no any/! /silent catch · finally resets · no dead code · BRAND_COLORS · path helpers · <500 line files · parity · 6 Arabic plurals · getTranslations server-side · logical props · a11y full checklist · sitemap/robots correctness · JSON-LD home only · next/image+font · pagination+select · BROWSER-VERIFIED interactivity (render, click, submit, real data, responsive, sticky footer, RTL, no console errors).

## N. Key Commands (Appendix A)
- Background dev server: `nohup setsid bash -c 'while true; do bun run dev >> dev.log 2>&1; sleep 3; done' < /dev/null > /dev/null 2>&1 & disown`
- `bun run lint` · `bunx tsc --noEmit` · `bun run db:push` (dev only) · `bun run db:seed` · `bunx prisma migrate deploy` (prod)
- i18n parity node one-liner (see guide) · find *.db (exactly one expected)

## O. Testing Priority (Appendix D — despite "no test code" default, pure logic verification matters)
Tier 1 pure functions first: overlaps/endOfDay boundary matrix, computeDays+tolerance, canTransition edges, brand path helpers. Tier 2 API integration (overbooking, price mismatch, webhook staleness/dup-nonce). Tier 3 one e2e smoke per critical path in BOTH locales.

## P. Context Notes (project-specific)
- The guide originates from a multi-tenant rental platform (brands: LUT #8B6B3D, LA_LOUNGE #E6007E, YOUR_BIRTHDAY #FFCC00; KWD currency, 3 decimals; old color #F5B914 is stale)
- The current sandbox project root: /home/z/my-project (Next.js 16 ready, Prisma configured, shadcn/ui present)
- Sandbox constraint: only route visible to user is `/` in src/app/page.tsx; API-first (use api routes, not server actions, per environment rules); port 3000 only; dev.log monitoring mandatory
- Full original guide remains available at: /home/z/my-project/upload/FULLSTACK_AGENT_GUIDE (2).md

Task ID: AUDIT-FIX-1
Agent: Main (Z.ai Code)
Task: Execute all fixes from the Phase-1 audit report (elyra-phase1-audit.md): P0-1 mandatory + all 14 P1 items + extra defects found during browser verification.

Work Log:
- **P0-1 (ESLint)**: re-enabled React 19 strict rules in `eslint.config.mjs` (`@typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any|no-non-null-assertion|no-unused-vars`, `react-hooks/exhaustive-deps|purity|immutability`, `react-compiler/react-compiler: warn`); installed `eslint-plugin-react-compiler@19.1.0-rc.2`. Fixed all 19 surfaced errors:
  - hero-canvas.tsx: particle geometry `useMemo` → `useState` lazy initializer (Math.random legality per react-hooks/purity).
  - navbar.tsx: removed dead `isHome`.
  - cta.tsx: `className` prop now applied to section.
  - automation-simulator.tsx: removed dead `elapsedMs` state + `elapsed` accumulator.
  - sidebar.tsx: random width useMemo → useState; justified eslint-disable for vendored cookie write (react-compiler).
  - use-toast.ts: `actionTypes as const` → direct readonly type alias.
  - hero-canvas/bento/work/automation pages: unused imports removed.
- **P1-1**: global-error.tsx bilingual (ar/en) via `useSyncExternalStore` browser-language detection (set-state-in-effect-safe), `<html lang dir>` dynamic.
- **P1-2**: loading.tsx → async server component with `getTranslations('common')` (key `common.loading` existed).
- **P1-3**: prisma schema: dropped template User/Post → added `Lead` model (calculator/contact capture, @@index([status, createdAt]) + @@index([email])); `bun run db:push` + `db:generate` OK.
- **P1-4**: cta.tsx Link gets `group` class (arrow hover works).
- **P1-5**: removed redundant aria-label from cta.tsx section (h2 names it) and work-grid.tsx tablist (self-describing tabs).
- **P1-6**: MiniAgent typewriter honors `useReducedMotion` — instant full text, no pulsing cursor.
- **P1-7**: MiniCube dead `{reduced ? null : null}` removed; `reduced` now disables the rotation easing transition.
- **P1-8**: single-flip arrows everywhere — `ArrowRight + rtl:rotate-180` for forward links (cta/hero/page-hero/featured-work); calculator back=`ArrowLeft`/next=`ArrowRight`, each with one `rtl:rotate-180`.
- **P1-9**: Footer year hydration-safe via `useSyncExternalStore` (server snapshot 2026) — better than audit's suggested useEffect+setState which violates `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect`.
- **P1-10**: added `footer.social.whatsapp` key (ar: واتساب / en: WhatsApp); aria-label localized.
- **P1-11**: added `nav.ariaLabel` key (القائمة الرئيسية / Main navigation); navbar uses it.
- **P1-12**: mobile Sheet `side={isRtl ? 'left' : 'right'}` — verified opens from left in AR.
- **P1-13**: before-after slider fully RTL-mirrored: pos measured from START edge, clip `inset(0 pos% 0 0)` in RTL, handle anchored `right:`, pointer math mirrored; keyboard semantics already RTL-correct.
- **P1-14**: created `src/lib/site-config.ts` (SITE_CONTACT + SITE_SOCIAL + BRAND_COLORS); footer, contact page, and home JSON-LD now read from it. ⚠️ Values remain PLACEHOLDERS — real WhatsApp number/social accounts still required before launch.
- **Bonus fix 1**: React 19 hydration false-positive in MiniOrbit (CSSOM normalizes `calc(50% + -24.00000000000002px - 14px)` → `calc(50% - 38px)`); positions now pre-computed at module scope, rounded to 4 decimals, formatted in CSSOM canonical form.
- **Bonus fix 2**: `/icon` 404 — root cause: content-hash lives in query string (no path extension) so the next-intl matcher didn't exclude `/icon` and rewrote it into [locale]. Added `icon` to proxy.ts matcher exclusions + removed manual `icons:{icon:'/icon'}` from layout metadata (convention link is hashed) + removed `runtime='edge'` from icon.tsx. Favicon now serves a valid PNG.
- **Bonus fix 3**: `/robots.txt` 500 — conflicting template leftover `public/robots.txt` vs dynamic `src/app/robots.ts`. Deleted the static file (audit wrongly claimed no conflict existed).

Stage Summary:

- `bunx tsc --noEmit` = 0 errors

Work Log:
- **Magnetic Cursor** (`src/components/sensory/magnetic-cursor.tsx`): two layers (6px dot exact + 32px ring lerp 0.2) driven by ONE rAF loop writing `translate3d` via refs (zero React re-renders). Magnet snap within 80px of `[data-cursor="magnet"]` centers (distance-based → RTL/LTR neutral). Guards: `pointer:fine` + NOT reduced-motion + client-only. Native cursor hidden only under those conditions (double CSS media guard). Layers: `pointer-events:none`, `aria-hidden`, z-200 ABOVE Sheet/Dialog (native hidden → must stay visible over modals), `mix-blend-difference` for light/dark visibility. Magnet rects cached, refreshed on scroll/resize (rAF-throttled) + debounced MutationObserver for client navigations. Press feedback (scale 0.82) on pointerdown.

Task ID: PHASE-5

Work Log:

- **WS-1 (P0-1)** — WebGL scene rendering invisible after preset click:
  - Root cause: `<line>` JSX intrinsic collides with SVG line in R3F v9 + React 19 → silent render failure (mask-image:none, 0 non-zero pixels in canvas).
  - Fix: rewrote ConsoleScene to construct THREE.Line instances via `<primitive>` (bypasses the JSX collision). Per-preset layout (ring/orbit/flow) so each architecture reads distinctly. Brightened lighting + pushed camera back per preset. Added InitialRenderSafety component that triggers an extra invalidation on mount.
  - Also added auto-scroll: the dark scene container lives below the fold on standard screens, so clicking a preset never actually revealed the canvas. Now `scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' })` on preset click/Enter.
  - Verified visually with VLM on 4 presets (store/booking/ai/dashboard) + custom free-text. Clarity 8/10 on the matched snapshot pattern (was 0/10).

- **WS-2 (P0-2)** — Spotlight + Blueprint grid invisible:
  - Three root causes combined: (a) `radial-gradient(700px circle at ...)` reversed the canonical shape-then-size order → modern Chromium silently rejected mask-image → grid leaked across whole viewport; (b) grid alpha 4% was too low for VLM detection; (c) no default state — grid invisible until first mousemove.
  - Fix: canonical syntax `circle 400px at var(--mx,50%) var(--my,50%)`, alpha raised 4% → 22%, dim default (opacity 0.45) until first pointermove, then `.spotlight-active` class ramps to 1. Default position center 50% 50% so first-time visitors see a hint.
  - Verified with full Chromium (channel:'chromium' — agent-browser's headless defaults to (hover:none) which triggers the touch fallback): mask-image applies correctly, grid visible in a focused ~400px circular beam under the cursor. VLM 7/10 (was 0/10).

- **WS-3 (P1-1)** — Mobile + desktop active link state:
  - Mobile menu: original 2px (h-0.5) primary bar at the bottom was lost in the rounded-xl container. Now: 4px (w-1) primary bar on the START side (RTL-correct via 'inset-y-2 start-0') + bg-primary/15 + ring-1 ring-primary/40 + text-primary + soft glow shadow.
  - Desktop navbar: original 20% opacity text shift was invisible. Now: primary underline that scales in (opacity 0→100% + scale-x 0→100%) on the active link, with a hover-only 50% half-bar on inactive links.
  - Verified: VLM 9/10 on both mobile + desktop, active link instantly identifiable (was 0.85/10).

- **WS-4 (P1-2)** — Deconstructed card 3D layer separation:
  - Three root causes: (a) outer 'h-screen max-h-[800px]' (800px) was SHORTER than the inner 'h-screen' (100vh) → sticky never engaged (sticky duration = outer - viewport = negative); (b) card was the FIRST item in a md:grid-cols-2 grid → grid cell constrained 200vh outer down to ~240px (row's natural height); (c) layer Z values all-positive (0/+28/+56) → depth delta only 56px with perspective 1000 → too subtle for VLM detection.
  - Fix: outer 'h-[200vh]' gives 100vh of sticky scroll time. Pulled the DeconstructedCard OUT of the grid as a full-width block before the grid. Layer Z spread AROUND 0: back=-50/middle=0/front=+50 at p=1. Perspective 1000→1500. Distinct visual language per layer (browser frame / n8n nodes / metric slides) with colored borders + matching shadows.
  - Verified: sticky zone now engages at the card's natural position (y≈3900). Transforms progress linearly from translateZ(0px) at scroll start to translateZ(-49.7px) back / 0 middle / +49.7px front at end. VLM 7/10 (was 0.7/10).

- **WS-5 (P1-3 + P1-4)** — Contact whitespace + calculator rings:
  - P1-3: removed the redundant 133px-tall dark CTA band between channels and calculator (it duplicated the calculator's kicker+title — the Calculator component already renders its own SectionHeading). Page height reduced 2770→2637px (-5%).
  - P1-4 bug 1: animated value was the wrong number. The original RingGauge animated displayValue from 0 to 'fraction' (a 0-1 number), then formatted that as money via formatValue(n). So a $15000 budget with fraction=0.75 showed as '$1'. Fix: split 'fraction' (ring fill, 0-1) from 'value' (count-up display, the actual money/weeks number). Calculator now passes BOTH: fraction=Math.min(1, result.max/20000) AND value=result.max.
  - P1-4 bug 2: animation never ran in React Strict Mode. The original useEffect set fromRef.current = to BEFORE the rAF actually fired. Strict Mode runs effect setup → cleanup → effect setup again. First setup updated fromRef but its cleanup cancelled the rAF before it could tick. Second setup saw from === to → early-returned → no animation at all → rings stayed at 0 forever. Fix: only commit fromRef.current = to INSIDE the tick callback when the animation completes. Added a 'cancelled' flag so the cleanup can signal the in-flight tick to bail without scheduling another rAF.
  - Verified: rings animate 0 → $6,000 / 6 weeks over 800ms. VLM 9/10 on the result step (was 0/10 — VLM said 'no rings visible').

- **WS-6 (deferred)** — Kinetic typography (cursor-velocity → font weight):
  - New hook useCursorVelocity (src/lib/use-cursor-velocity.ts): tracks pointermove, computes velocity (px/ms) per frame, maps velocity → wght (0 speed → 600, saturation 2.5 px/ms → 800, idle after 200ms → 700), Lerp 0.18 toward target, writes --wght straight to the target element's style via ref (zero React re-renders per frame). Activation guards: pointer:fine + NOT reduced-motion + client mount.
  - New component KineticHeading (src/components/home/kinetic-heading.tsx): server-renders h1 with static wght 700 (LCP-safe, no flash), then hook attaches and writes --wght after hydration. Splits each title line into per-word inline-block spans (Arabic shaping benefits from per-word isolation — complex-contextual shaping doesn't bleed across the whole line). h1 binds font-variation-settings to var(--wght, 700).
  - Hero h1 wired to KineticHeading (range 600→800, was fixed wght 200 — too thin). Primary CTA also wired (narrower range 600→700) — feels responsive to cursor speed without competing with the h1.
  - Verified: initial wght 700, slow movement drops to ~637-660 (lighter), idle returns to ~698-700, fast zigzag rises to 730+ (heavier). Per-word spans confirmed in both AR + EN. VLM on fast-motion screenshot: 'bold/heavy (700-800+ weight), highly readable'.

- **WS-7 (deepening)** — .card-light-deep + 3 new data-cursor contexts:
  - New .card-light-deep CSS class (counterpart to .card-deep): 4-6% black border over light + multi-layer shadow (2px+8px+20px offsets) + 1px white top-highlight inset + 0.6 white bg + 8px backdrop blur. Contrast-safe: depth adds WITHOUT darkening → text on white stays ≥ 4.5:1.
  - Three new data-cursor contexts (zoom/inspect/external): magnetic-cursor now resolves each context's chip label from the common.cursor.* i18n catalog (AR + EN). Elements with explicit data-cursor-label still override (back-compat preserved). Chip element gets dir='auto' so the chip text flips correctly in RTL/LTR.
  - Applied: footer social icons (5 elements) → external ('رابط خارجي' / 'External link'). Work page project cards (6 elements) → zoom. Automation simulator stage → inspect ('افحص العنصر' / 'Inspect element'). All chips verified to show the translated label at opacity 1 on hover.
  - Layout fix: MagneticCursor moved INSIDE NextIntlClientProvider so the new useTranslations('common.cursor') call resolves at runtime. Previously it was outside the provider → 'context not found' error.
  - i18n parity 511→515 keys (+4: cursor.zoom/inspect/external/magnet empty string for magnet).

- **WS-8 (production readiness)** — Reduce initial JS via lazy-loading Calculator + Methodology:
  - New CalculatorLazy + MethodologyLazy wrappers (next/dynamic ssr:false + IO with rootMargin 400px + section-shaped placeholder for zero CLS). Both framer-motion-using components deferred until near viewport.
  - Result: framer-motion's ~30KB minified+gzipped chunk + calculator + methodology component chunks now load ONLY when the user scrolls toward them, not at initial page load. Verified via chunk tracing: 0 framer/calculator/methodology chunks at initial networkidle, 4 such chunks loaded after scroll (methodology_tsx ×2, calculator_tsx ×1, framer-motion ×1).
  - LCP-safe: both components well below the fold. All 6 routes (×AR/EN) still render. Zero console/page errors.

Now looks for 'header button[aria-label*=المؤثرات/sound]'. Score 8/10 → 9/10 (remaining Three.js-at-load check is a pre-existing test timing artifact — requestIdleCallback fires within networkidle's window in playwright).

- **WS-9 (P2)** — Raise secondary-text contrast on light:

Work Log:
- FIX 1 (P1-1) route.ts: clientIp() now env-gated — TRUST_PROXY !== 'true' (default) ignores X-Forwarded-For/x-real-ip entirely and keys the limiter on the literal 'anonymous' (fail-closed shared bucket); TRUST_PROXY=true keeps leftmost-XFF → x-real-ip → 'anonymous'. ipForRecord nulls the 'anonymous' sentinel. Doc comment explains when to set true.
- FIX 1 (P1-1) lib/rate-limit.ts: sweep() now hard-caps the hits Map — after pruning, if size > 5_000 it deletes the oldest 1_000 entries (Map insertion order) to bound memory under key-flooding.
- FIX 2 (P1-2) route.ts: pre-parse cheap header gates — content-length > 64 KiB → 413 {error:'too_large', message:t('tooLarge')}; content-type must contain application/json → else 415 (generic 'invalid' shape). Both before req.json().
- FIX 3 (P1-3): git rm --cached db/custom.db (file kept on disk, app unaffected); .gitignore gains db/*.db, db/*.db-wal, db/*.db-shm (!.env.example exception untouched). History NOT rewritten — 5 commits still carry the blob but it contains only synthetic test data (verified by L1-A audit via strings extraction); noted here as the permanent record.
- FIX 4 (P1-4) lib/db.ts: Prisma log is now env-conditional — production: ['error']; else ['query','error','warn'] (PII: lead names/emails/phones must not reach prod logs).

HSTS: dropped 'preload' (kept max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains).
- FIX 7 (P2-1) lib/n8n-webhook.ts: retry now regenerates ts/nonce/HMAC per attempt (attemptDelivery() builds fresh headers inside the loop); 5s AbortController timeout per attempt, single retry, fire-and-forget semantics and all exported signatures preserved.

Real-world impact bounded to ~12 tiny geometries and fully recovered by R3F Canvas context teardown on unmount (no persistent GPU leak); the code comment's stated invariant ("final cleanup disposes the current set") is factually wrong. Fix: dispose the `lines` closure value in the cleanup (same StrictMode semantics as the accepted capability-scene geo/mat pattern). Verified it can NOT dispose still-mounted objects (the Loop 1 concern) — it errs toward under-disposal.
- Otherwise CLEAN: no hooks violations, no hydration errors, no unpaired lifecycles, no races with real consequences, no React 19 anti-patterns, dev.log clean.

  - Methods: GET/PUT 405, OPTIONS 204 (unauthenticated, static — fine).
  - Validation torture: pages 0/-1/21/1.5/null/true/"5"/1e999 -> 400; 20/1e0 -> 201 (correct); integrations 7 items/dupes/mixed/non-array -> 400; source field-confusion both directions -> 400; client-echo estimate forgery -> stripped + 201; name 101 chars -> 400; message 5000 OK; email no-TLD/newline -> 400; 254-char email OK.

  - Rate limit: same key 6th -> 429 + Retry-After 60; XFF rotation burst (12 fresh keys <1s) all 201 (documented sandbox artifact: TRUST_PROXY=true + direct :3000; the TLS proxy overwrites XFF on the prod path); header-case -> same bucket; IPv6 bracket/canonical forms = distinct keys (same artifact); XFF-absent requests get XFF=::1 INJECTED by the dev server (probe: X-Real-IP-only request stored ip=::1, not the header value — x-real-ip fallback is dead code in dev; prod standalone follows code as written).
  - Honeypot matrix: absent/empty -> real store; whitespace-only -> REAL store (trim-before-check nuance); 200 chars -> fake 201 (no row); 201 chars -> 400. Honeypot-vs-real timing delta ~1-2ms localhost — negligible.
  - Header attacks: 20KB header -> 431 (Node cap); 70KB body -> 413; chunked TE 80KB -> 413 (Loop-2 fix verified live); CL:0-lie -> 400; CL 999999 -> 413; dup CL -> 400; dup CT -> 201; x-elyra-locale fr/AR/garbage/500-char/script-tag -> harmless fallback (no reflection).
  - Origin/Sec-Fetch: evil Origin -> 403; matching Origin -> 201; cross-site/same-site -> 403; same-origin -> 201; malformed Origin -> 403; attacker-controlled Host+Origin pair passes (non-browser client, already allowed by design).
  - 500 hunt: deep 15k nesting -> 400; array/null/string bodies -> 400; __proto__/constructor keys -> 400 (no prototype pollution; out['__proto__'] assignment no-ops on plain object); no stack traces leaked; dev.log PII-free.
  - Webhook: N8N_* unset in .env -> "webhook disabled" per stored lead (74 log lines); URL is env-only — client cannot influence target or firing; honeypot path never fires it.
  - Crawl: robots/sitemap clean; /admin /.env /db/custom.db /.git/* /messages/* /scripts/* /Dockerfile /%2e%2e traversal -> all 404.

Fix: add message String? column + persist stored.message.

No enumeration risk (no lookup endpoint) but duplicate customer handles. Fix: random per-row reference.

Pushed schema to db/custom.db (additive nullable column, non-destructive; PrismaClient regenerated) and fixed the stale "Phase 3 will wire" schema header to present tense (R2-LOW-3).
- GOTCHA (documented for everyone): the Prisma CLI resolves RELATIVE SQLite URLs against prisma/schema.prisma, NOT the CWD — `DATABASE_URL="file:./db/custom.db" bunx prisma db push` from the repo root creates prisma/db/custom.db (a phantom empty DB) and leaves db/custom.db untouched (verified live by hash). Pushed with the .env absolute URL instead; deleted the stray prisma/db/.
- FIX2 (HIGH R6): Dockerfile build stage now runs `mkdir -p db && DATABASE_URL="file:/app/db/custom.db" bunx prisma db push --skip-generate` after `bun run build` (ABSOLUTE URL because of the relative-path gotcha above — the task's literal `file:./db/custom.db` would land at /app/prisma/db/custom.db); runtime stage gains `COPY --from=build /app/db ./db` right after the .prisma copy, BEFORE the Loop-2 chown → fresh named volumes seed the initialized schema from the image, first insert can't P2021. Chain reasoned (docker unavailable in sandbox): prisma CLI is present in the build stage (devDeps), runtime needs no CLI since the DB ships initialized.
- FIX3 (LOW R1): reference is now `'c' + 9 crypto.randomInt(36)` base36 chars via randomReference() — used by BOTH the real 201 and the honeypot fake 201 (shapes identical, 10 chars now); no more timestamp-bucket collisions.
- FIX4 (LOW R1): isConfigured() fails closed in NODE_ENV=production when the webhook URL is not https:// — loud console.warn "lead PII would be sent in cleartext; refusing to enable", treated as not configured; dev http://localhost stays allowed.
- FIX5 (LOW R1): neutralizeCsvInjection() (OWASP CSV injection) prefixes `'` when a trimmed lead field (name/email/whatsapp/message) starts with = + - @ — applied ONLY to the outbound webhook payload (DB keeps raw); the HMAC signature is computed over the sanitized body so receiver verification stays consistent.
- FIX6: route.ts imports leadNameSchema/leadEmailSchema/leadWhatsappSchema/leadMessageSchema/honeypotSchema from @/lib/lead-fields (baseFields + contactLeadSchema.message); removed the duplicated WHATSAPP_PATTERN + z.email comment from route.ts. Cc/Cf refine issues surface on the right field key (verified live: RLO name → 400 fields.name).
- FIX7 (LOW R2): removed dead resetRateLimiter export from rate-limit.ts (grep: zero importers — only worklog mentions).
- FIX8 (LOW R2): db:push safety split — "db:push": "prisma db push" (safe default) + "db:push:force": "prisma db push --accept-data-loss". Rationale: the old default was one env-var/schema drift away from silently dropping the Lead table (SQLite push can recreate tables); --accept-data-loss now requires a deliberate, explicit opt-in.

Stage Summary:

Chip click carries any typed prompt text as `idea` (typed-then-chipped is one intent). Navigation fires AFTER a payoff beat (800ms; 250ms under prefers-reduced-motion) so the scene still mounts and words materialize — chose beat-over-immediate because the scene mounts ONLY on submit, so instant navigation would render the 231KB WebGL scene unreachable dead code. Timer in a cancellable ref (unmount-safe, double-submit guarded, reset after fire so Back+retry works).

Agent: Group3-Signature-3D

Work Log:
- Read worklog tail (3-convergence contract, 4-I1, 4-I2, loops2-G1+G2 G1-d/G2-c findings) + all four target files + hero.tsx (deferred mount / IO-gated active), hero-fallback & --background-deep tokens (#08080A), eslint config, three@0.185.1 exports map, @types/three RoomEnvironment.d.ts.
- src/components/home/hero-canvas.tsx (items 12+13+15, full motion rework): replaced per-particle sin/cos jitter with a proper 3D simplex CURL field — Ashima snoise (canonical, MIT) + snoiseVec3 + finite-difference curl (6 samples, safe-normalized, divergence-free => neighbouring particles share velocity => streaming ribbons). Vertex math: q = seed*0.16 + time-translating offset (field structures sweep past volume), p += curl*amp (amp = 1.1+aSpeed*1.6, swelled ×(1+uScroll*0.4)); slow global drift = embers rising on wrapped y-domain (mod, DOMAIN 20) with edge fade smoothstep(8.5,10) masking the recycle seam; kept uMouse*0.18 attraction, distance fade, soft-round additive sprites, point-size clamp. Count 3500→4500 (still 1 draw call, same buffer attributes + lazy useState initializer). COLORS → #F59E0B/#FBBF24/#34A853/#10B981/#FFF3D6 (weighted, zero blues). Uniforms: uTime/uMouse/uPixelRatio/uScroll (4).
- Aurora backdrop INSIDE the same Canvas (no second Canvas, mounts with the chunk): 64×32 plane at z=-9, renderOrder -1, depthWrite/depthTest false, 4-octave value-noise fbm (rotated octaves), two drifting amber/green curtains + warm overlap core + broad cursor wash (world-space projected uMouse, aspect-correct) + vignette on the light. CRITICAL blend fix: transparent canvas + plain AdditiveBlending would accumulate alpha≈1 over the fullscreen plane and HIDE the CSS stage — used CustomBlending (blendSrc/blendDst ONE/ONE, blendSrcAlpha/blendDstAlpha ZERO/ONE) so canvas alpha stays 0 = true additive light OVER the page: deep bg, hero-fallback gradient AND the WS-3 blueprint spotlight grid stay visible beneath.
- Scroll dolly (item 15): new src/lib/hero-scroll.ts — module-level bridge { bindHeroScroll(mv): unbind, getHeroScroll(): number } (getter guards undefined → 0). methodology.tsx: ONE useEffect line feeds its existing useScroll scrollYProgress into the bridge (+2 import tweaks; no restructure). hero-canvas: ScrollDolly component eases camera z 6→6.8 via state.camera (useFrame param — avoids the useThree-return immutability error), RTL-safe (view-axis only); Particles lerp uScroll from the same bridge.
- capability-scene.tsx (item 14): RoomEnv component — import { RoomEnvironment } from 'three/examples/jsm/environments/RoomEnvironment.js' (verified: file exists in three@0.185.1, './examples/jsm/*' in exports map, @types/three ships the .d.ts; no new dependency). useEffect: PMREMGenerator(gl).fromScene(new RoomEnvironment(), 0.04).texture → scene.environment (eslint-disable react-hooks/immutability, canonical three IBL wiring); disposes room + target + pmrem on unmount. Lights untouched (I-1 amber/green/warm-white). envMapIntensity already 1 on knot/satellites.
- Also added ContextLossGuard to hero-canvas (mirrors capability-scene — KEEP list).
- Verification: scoped `bunx eslint` on all 4 files → 0 errors (1 benign react-compiler warn, same class as existing convention). NO tsc (coordinator gates centrally). - Verification: `bunx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0. `bun run lint` → exit 0, zero errors AND zero warnings (no new react-compiler warns — all useFrame mutations go through ref.current or carry the repo's immutability disable). Two-level IQ domain warp: q = fbm(p + vec2(0.6t, −0.4t)), r = fbm(p·1.3 + vec2(q·1.7, q·1.1) + vec2(−0.5t, 0.3t) + 2.7), f = fbm(p + r·2.2), t = uTime·0.1 → effective offsets 0.06/−0.04/−0.05/0.03 units/s counter-drifting (fields shear → folds re-form, never loop). Exactly 3 fbm calls, only loop is fbm's, no derivatives, GLSL1 only.
- Mouse vortex: pos = uMouse + rot(0.35·exp(−md²/24))·(vPos−uMouse) — gaussian stir radius ≈6 world units, ≤0.35 rad at centre, total displacement < ~0.7 units (composition stays calm); uMouse still CPU-lerped world-space projection (0.05). Broad cursor wash kept but now blue-family only (mix(uColorC,uColorA,0.75)·exp(−md²/220)·0.10) since green is reserved for filaments.
- Colour: uColorA #4285F4 body hue-graded by q through derived midBlue mix(C,A,0.55)≈#0071E3; lightBlue mix(A,white,0.22)≈#60A5FA ridge sheen; NEW uColorC #0A2A5E undertone in low-energy zones (deep² weighting, coeff 0.30); uColorB #34A853 confined to thin filaments = narrow level band of the r field (smoothstep(0.46,0.55)·(1−smoothstep(0.60,0.70))) × body × sheen. Uniforms object: ADDED uColorC + uScroll only (backwards-compatible shape).
- Intensity discipline: hard per-channel governor col = min(col, vec3(0.42)); body coeff 0.16+0.12·ridge, ridge sheen 0.20, filaments 0.40, undertone 0.30, wash 0.10. Old aurora's uncapped peak was ~0.54 linear blue on double-curtain cores — the new cap is strictly darker; large-area average ≈0.04–0.06 linear blue (moody). vert melt window kept verbatim; radial vignette kept (centre nudged 0.5→0.44) + lift 0.85–1.15 vertical bias so the silk is brightest in the upper-middle band behind the headline.
- uScroll wired in SilkBackdrop useFrame from getHeroScroll() with the same 0.08 lerp + eslint-disable react-hooks/immutability convention as Particles; shader swell = 1.0 + uScroll·0.25.
- Doc comments updated: file-header "Aurora backdrop (item 13)" paragraph rewritten to the silk model; SILK_FRAGMENT header comment describes warp structure/vortex/filaments; blending comment "beneath the silk".

- Verified: DOM (h1 3 lines correct, 6 masked words opacity 1, clock ticking ١٥:١٦:٣٨, 14 marquee items, watermark stroke, rail, orbit textPath, grain, no overflowX); manifesto progression (all dim at 86% scroll entry → 23/26 lit mid-read with fading tail + hidden signature → all lit + signature 1.0 at completion — after fixing the section-vs-title progress rect bug); console conversion path (typed "متجر عطور فاخرة" + Enter → /contact?service=store&idea=… → message prefilled "أرغب ببناء متجر ذكي: متجر عطور فاخرة" + whatsapp field + chip pressed); mobile 390×844 (no overflow, h1 43.2px full width, marquee 48px, console in fold, orbit badge correctly hidden); EN locale (dir ltr, "We Craft | Digital | Experiences", latin clock, gradient to right, 32 manifesto words); silk rendering (pixel-measured: blue-dominant 18%→61% after boost, right-upper mean (41,48,67), animation confirmed via 8s frame diff 30.3% subtle drift); VLM verdicts: hero wow 9/10 "Awwwards Site of the Day caliber, Arabic joining flawless, no diacritic clipping", manifesto 8/10 premium/intentional, mobile 8/10, background atmosphere 8/10 readable. Gates: tsc=0, lint=0. DB leads: 0.
- Custom cursor code-verified only (headless env reports pointer:none so it correctly stays disabled there; activates on real pointer:fine devices).
- Cleanup: deleted orphaned Phase-2 files (sensory/film-grain.tsx, sensory/magnetic-cursor.tsx — unmounted dead code superseded by R7-b components).

Stage Summary:
- Hero transformed from centered generic SaaS → asymmetric editorial-cinematic composition with masked word reveals, live Damascus clock, outlined watermark, vertical rail, orbit seal badge, service marquee edge, blend-difference cursor, film grain, magnetic CTAs, domain-warped silk WebGL backdrop, and a scroll-lit manifesto statement.
- Key architectural lessons: (1) .elyra-spotlight's content-lift rule forces position:relative on ALL non-bg-layer children — any new decorative absolute in spotlight sections MUST carry data-bg-layer; (2) mask-reveal line wrappers need flex-col parents to avoid negative-margin collapse; (3) additive shader intensity must be pixel-verified (VLM undersells subtle luminance; measure means + blue-dominance).
- Commits: this round's work committed as R7.

Stage Summary:
FINDINGS:

[P1] .git/config (environment; repo remote) — Live GitHub access token embedded in the origin remote URL
  Evidence: `git remote -v` → `https://x-access-token:<redacted>@github.com/ashamsaldin303-ctrl/nextjs-fullstack-project.git` (credential detected in the stored remote URL; value never printed).
  Impact: Any process, backup, CI log, or pasted `git remote -v` output that reads .git/config yields a usable GitHub credential for that repo (push/fetch scope unverified). It is a plaintext secret at rest on disk.
  Recommended fix: `git remote set-url origin https://github.com/ashamsaldin303-ctrl/nextjs-fullstack-project.git` and authenticate via credential helper or SSH deploy key instead; rotate the token if it was ever copied anywhere.

[P2] prisma/schema.prisma:15-17 + scripts/clean-leads.ts:20 + README Deployment — Lead PII (ipAddress, userAgent, email, whatsapp, message) retained indefinitely; promised retention policy does not exist
  Evidence: schema comment says "Define a retention policy before real production use (e.g., purge Lead rows older than 90 days) — see README 'Deployment'"; README has NO retention/purge text (rg 'retention|purge|90 days' → 0 hits) and clean-leads.ts only does `deleteMany({})` as a pre-launch full wipe; nothing is scheduled.
  Impact: Privacy/compliance exposure (IP + UA + full contact text are personal data under GDPR-like regimes); SQLite grows forever; a leaked DB file exposes the complete contact history.
  Recommended fix: Add an age-based purge job (cron/GitHub Action running `prisma.lead.deleteMany({ where: { createdAt: { lt: <cutoff> } } })`, or null out ipAddress/userAgent after 30–90 days keeping the business fields), and document the policy in README Deployment where the schema comment points.

[P2] Root reverse-proxy file :6-18 — Shipped proxy config contains an intentional open-proxy/SSRF primitive (?XTransformPort)
  Evidence: `reverse_proxy localhost:{query.XTransformPort}` proxies any request carrying `?XTransformPort=<port>` to an arbitrary localhost port; header block explicitly warns "SANDBOX-ONLY … NEVER deploy this publicly".
  Impact: An operator copy-pasting the repo's only provided proxy config into production exposes an SSRF relay into every localhost service on the host (databases, n8n, admin panels). The app's own TRUST_PROXY/XFF-overwrite guidance points users at this exact file.
  Recommended fix: Move the sandbox file to `sandbox/` (or gate the transform behind a build flag) and add a production-safe `deploy/` variant (TLS block, no XTransformPort, XFF overwrite only); mention the split in README Deployment.

[P2] route.ts:149-155 + rate-limit.ts:23-25 + Dockerfile:52-61 + README docker-run — Documented production deployment runs with TRUST_PROXY unset → single GLOBAL rate-limit bucket → trivial total lockout of the only conversion endpoint
  Evidence: `if (process.env.TRUST_PROXY !== 'true') return 'anonymous'` — with TRUST_PROXY unset/false every caller shares one bucket (30 lenient / 5 strict per MINUTE, globally). The Dockerfile ENV block does not set TRUST_PROXY and the README `docker run` example passes only DATABASE_URL/N8N_* vars; TRUST_PROXY appears in no deployment doc except a comment in the root proxy file (rg confirms: that comment + .env.example:31=false).
  Impact: ~30 junk POSTs/min from one curl loop keeps the shared lenient bucket permanently exhausted → every legitimate visitor gets 429 on BOTH forms site-wide, continuously. Availability of the lead pipeline hinges on an env var the canonical deploy recipe never sets.
  Recommended fix: Set `TRUST_PROXY=true` in the Dockerfile/README docker-run example (deployment is by definition behind the overwriting proxy), or reconsider the fail-closed default for the standalone Docker target; at minimum add TRUST_PROXY to the README run command with one explanatory line.

[P2] next.config.ts:26-38 (and 41-55) — Production CSP keeps 'unsafe-inline' in script-src; no COOP/CORP headers
  Evidence: `"script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'"` with the documented rationale (static prerender embeds inline bootstrap; nonce CSP incompatible). securityHeaders list has no Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy / Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy. (HSTS is emitted while the sandbox serves plain HTTP — harmless, browsers ignore it there; correct once TLS terminates at the proxy.)
  Impact: Any future HTML-injection bug escalates to full XSS with no CSP backstop (unsafe-inline voids script-src protection); missing COOP leaves the site without window.opener isolation. Current exploitability is LOW (React auto-escaping everywhere, no user-content HTML sink), so this is a hardening gap, not an active hole.
  Recommended fix: Add `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin` and `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin` now; evaluate hash-based CSP for the stable inline bootstrap chunks, or make the shell dynamic to enable nonce CSP. Keep the documented tradeoff note updated with whatever is chosen.

[P3] route.ts:287-305 — Body-size gate depends on content-length/transfer-encoding presence; length-less h2 forwarding would bypass it
  Evidence: 413 fires only when `content-length > 64 KB` or `transfer-encoding` is present; a request with NEITHER header (possible over HTTP/2 DATA frames) skips both gates and `req.json()` buffers the full body before Zod runs.
  Impact: Unreachable in the shipped stack (Node standalone = HTTP/1.1; the fronting proxy speaks h1 to upstream with CL/chunked), but a future h2c/h2-direct exposure turns this into a memory-DoS primitive.
  Recommended fix: Document "never expose the app over h2 directly / proxy must forward length framing", or wrap the parse with a size-checked stream read (e.g. read the body via a capped reader before JSON.parse).

[P3] git history (commits 6d367b6 → b659e9b) — .env was committed then deleted; history still contains it
  Evidence: `git log --all --name-status -- .env` → `A .env` in 6d367b6, `D .env` in b659e9b. Key names verified WITH VALUES REDACTED: it contained exactly one key, DATABASE_URL (local SQLite path — not a credential).
  Impact: Minimal today (no secret value), but the pattern is a footgun and the file remains retrievable from history if the repo goes public.
  Recommended fix: No action needed for a file path; if the repo will be public, rewrite history (git filter-repo/BFG) for hygiene and keep `.env*` ignored (already .gitignore:35).

[P3] n8n-webhook.ts:94-99 / :179 — deliver() takes a `secret` parameter it never uses
  Evidence: `async function deliver(url, secret, body, headers)` — the body of deliver() never reads `secret`; called as `deliver(config.url, config.secret, body, headers)`.
  Impact: No vulnerability (server-side only), but it needlessly propagates the HMAC secret through a call stack that doesn't need it — the kind of parameter that later gets logged by mistake.
  Recommended fix: Drop the `secret` parameter from deliver() and its call site.

[P3] rate-limit.ts:36-54 — Transient Map growth possible between throttled sweeps
  Evidence: `sweep()` executes at most once per 60 s (`if (now - lastSweep < SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS) return`); within that interval every NEW distinct key inserts without any size check; the 5 000-key cap only applies when the sweep body actually runs.
  Impact: Requires TRUST_PROXY=true AND direct reachability past the overwriting proxy (i.e., misdeployment) — an attacker minting fresh XFF keys can grow the Map unbounded for up to 60 s before the cap fires. Steady state is capped; realistic risk is low.
  Recommended fix: Cheap hardening — when inserting a key that didn't previously exist, also check `hits.size > 10_000` and run the eviction inline (or skip inserting the new key entirely).

[P3] robots.ts:9 — Disallow list advertises a nonexistent /admin path
  Evidence: `disallow: ['/api', '/admin']` — no admin route exists anywhere in the app (single API route, no GET, no admin pages).
  Impact: Cosmetic recon hint that an admin surface might exist; /api disallow is likewise cosmetic (endpoint is POST-only). No real leak — sitemap contains only the 12 public locale×path URLs.
  Recommended fix: Optional — drop `/admin` until an admin surface actually exists (counter-argument: keeping it pre-blocks a future panel from indexing; either choice is defensible, just be aware it advertises the path name).

[P3] src/components/seo/home-json-ld.tsx:14-55 — JSON-LD embedded via dangerouslySetInnerHTML without `</script>` escaping
  Evidence: `dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify({...}) }}` — JSON.stringify does NOT escape `</script>`, so any value containing it would break out of the script element. All current inputs are build-time constants (SITE_URL env, SITE_CONTACT/SITE_SOCIAL literals).
  Impact: Safe as shipped (no user-controlled data reaches it), but the pattern breaks silently the day a dynamic value is added.
  Recommended fix: Wrap with `.replace(/</g, '\\u003c')` on the stringified output (standard JSON-LD hardening).

[P3] Repo tree — Internal agent guides/prompts and a wildcard-CORS example are tracked in git
  Evidence: tracked: `upload/elyra-fullstack-prompt-01..05.md`, `upload/FULLSTACK_AGENT_GUIDE (2).md`, `upload/elyra-phase1-audit.md`, `download/README.md`, `examples/websocket/{server,frontend}.ts` (server.ts:8-11 uses `cors: { origin: "*" }`, no auth — inert example, not imported by the app, absent from the Docker runtime image). Also `tool-results/` is currently untracked but NOT ignored (same artifact class was purged before in commit 887c4dc).
  Impact: Combined with the P1 remote credential: if this repo is or ever becomes public, internal architecture/audit documentation is exposed; the example could be copy-pasted as-is into a real service.
  Recommended fix: Verify repo visibility is private; move internal docs out of the tracked tree (or a private docs repo); add `tool-results/` to .gitignore; label examples/ clearly as non-production.

Verified-safe (no action): CSRF layering (Sec-Fetch-Site cross-site/same-site 403 + Origin/Host match + 415 content-type gate blocks form-post spam before the origin check); honeypot returns an indistinguishable fake 201 before the strict bucket (bots can't fingerprint or burn quota); server-side estimate recompute with client-echo field stripping; webhook URL is env-only (no SSRF via user input), HTTPS-enforced in production, secret-length-gated, HMAC-signed with fresh timestamp/nonce per attempt, 5 s AbortController timeout, one network-only retry, failures server-log-only and never alter the 201; Prisma usage is a single typed create (no raw SQL, no read endpoint); production Prisma logging is error-only (no PII query logs); rate limiter is synchronous on a single-threaded runtime (no sliding-window race), blocked requests don't extend the window, Retry-After computed from the oldest hit; reference IDs use crypto.randomInt (collision-proof, no row-id leak); client forms surface only translated catalog strings; API error responses never echo values, stacks, or internal paths (unknown-field NAMES are echoed as JSON keys — inert); zod 4.3.5 schemas are strict, bounded, and ReDoS-free (all regexes linear/anchored); OG/apple icons render static content on cached metadata routes (no per-request render cost); Dockerfile runs non-root with no .env in the runtime image and secrets via -e.

VERDICT: 0 P0, 1 P1, 4 P2, 7 P3 — The leads pipeline itself is genuinely well-engineered (strict bounded validation, layered CSRF, honeypot, signed webhook, clean error hygiene); the real risks are operational: a plaintext GitHub token in the git remote, an undocumented PII retention gap, a sandbox proxy config carrying an open-proxy primitive, and a default deployment posture (TRUST_PROXY unset) that lets one trivial script globally 429 the only conversion endpoint.

Stage Summary:
FINDINGS:

[P2] src/app/api/leads/route.ts:430 + prisma/schema.prisma — Customer reference is never persisted
  Evidence: `const reference = randomReference()` is generated AFTER `db.lead.create(...)`, included only in the 201 response + webhook payload; the Lead model has no `reference` column (only id/name/email/whatsapp/message/service/pages/... — schema.prisma:22-46). The success UI shows "رقمك المرجعي: {reference}" (calculator.form.successReference), and .env.example ships with N8N_WEBHOOK_URL empty (webhook disabled by default).
  Impact: The reference shown to every customer is untraceable — the team cannot map "c5tr8p13xb" to a stored row (CRM lookup fails; with the webhook disabled the reference exists NOWHERE except the user's toast). Support/repeat-contact workflows break.
  Recommended fix: Add `reference String @unique` to the Lead model; generate the reference BEFORE `db.lead.create` and include it in `data` (keep the 'c'+9 base36 format); optionally catch P2002 unique violation and regenerate (odds ~1e-14, one retry is plenty). While touching the schema, document the `service:'contact'` sentinel (route.ts:246) in a schema comment.

[P2] src/app/[locale]/opengraph-image.tsx:11-13,44 + src/app/not-found.tsx:48,60 — Blue-palette revert leftovers: amber remnants + comments asserting the abolished "no blue" law
  Evidence: opengraph-image.tsx:11-12 comment claims "quad-dot brand language (amber/gold/red/green — palette law: no blue)" while :80/:156 draw #4285F4 blue dots and :44 still has the amber-era radial `rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35)`; root not-found.tsx:48 chip hover border `rgba(245,158,11,.5)` + :60 radial `rgba(245,158,11,0.16)` sit next to the blue CTA #0071E3 (:125) and focus ring #4285F4 (:49). Current global law is blue (--primary #0071E3, --g-blue #4285F4 — globals.css:71,86; user round R1-R6 reverted the amber palette).
  Impact: The social-shared OG card and the last-resort 404 mix amber glow/hover with blue brand accents (off-brand vs the restored identity); the stale "palette law: no blue" comments actively mislead the next editor into "fixing" the blue dots back to amber.
  Recommended fix: Decide the accent once: either swap the three rgba(245,158,11,*) to the blue family (e.g. rgba(0,113,227,0.35)/.5/.16) for full consistency, or keep a deliberate warm secondary — then update BOTH comments to state the actual current decision. (1-line CSS + 2 comment edits per file.)

[P3] messages/ar.json + en.json — 8 dead catalog keys (present in both locales, consumed by nothing)
  Evidence: namespace-aware t()-literal scan + direct grep across src/ shows zero references to: `nav.services` (navbar consumes websites/automation/work/about/contact/home/cta/ariaLabel/openMenu/closeMenu only), `common.back`, `common.next` (calculator uses its own calculator.back/next), `common.close`, `common.getStarted`, `common.explore`, `common.reduceMotionNote`, `hero.canvasFallback`.

[P3] scripts/check-i18n-parity.js:1-111 — Parity gate has three blind spots
  Evidence: The script verifies presence/type/array-length/placeholder-set only. It cannot distinguish intentional empties from accidents (`common.cursor.magnet` = "" in both files passes silently — intentional per custom-cursor.tsx:42, but nothing encodes that), never flags ar==en values ( untranslated-copy risk — currently all 42 identical values are legitimate numbers/brand strings), and has no usage-vs-catalog check (the 8 dead keys above exist today).
  Impact: False confidence — an accidentally blanked or untranslated string ships silently.
  Recommended fix: Extend the script: (a) empty-string check with allowlist ['common.cursor.magnet'], (b) warn-list of identical ar/en string values, (c) optional unused-key scan (same t()-literal heuristic used in this audit).

[P3] src/lib/n8n-webhook.ts:94-99 — `deliver()` declares an unused `secret` parameter
  Evidence: `async function deliver(url: string, secret: string, body: string, headers: ...)` — `secret` is never read inside the function body (signing happens in attemptDelivery); caller passes config.secret (:179).
  Impact: Reader confusion — the signature implies the transport layer authenticates with the secret, which it does not. (Passes lint only because of after-used arg semantics.)
  Recommended fix: Drop the parameter (and the argument at :179) or rename to `_secret`.

[P3] src/lib/seo.ts:50 vs src/app/[locale]/layout.tsx:88 — Inconsistent OG image alt text

[P3] src/app/[locale]/layout.tsx:98-100 — SITE_URL re-derived instead of reusing the import
  Evidence: Line 8 imports SITE_URL from '@/lib/seo' (used for ogImage), but metadataBase is built from a duplicate expression `new URL(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL ?? 'http://localhost:3000')`.
  Impact: Two sources of truth for the same value (drift risk if the fallback ever changes in seo.ts).

[P1] capability-scene.tsx:462 — Mouse parallax can never activate (R3F hijacks e.currentTarget)
  Evidence: `const rect = (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).getBoundingClientRect?.()` … `if (rect) { mouse.current.tx = … }` — in @react-three/fiber v9 the raycast event overrides BOTH target and currentTarget with `{ hasPointerCapture, setPointerCapture, releasePointerCapture }` (verified in node_modules/@react-three/fiber/dist/events-156d8d12.esm.js:765-773); that object has NO getBoundingClientRect, so `rect` is always undefined and the only writes to mouse.current.tx/ty (lines 464-465) never execute. The useFrame parallax (lines 416-421) then lerps toward (0,0) forever.
  Impact: The documented "mouse parallax lerp (now on a parent group so blob+satellites+halo drift together)" is a dead feature — the scene never responds to hover position (only auto-spin + drag still work).
  Recommended fix: Drop manual rect math entirely — read the R3F-maintained NDC pointer inside useFrame: `const { gl, pointer } = state; parallax.current.position.x += (pointer.x * viewport.width / 4 - parallax.current.position.x) * 0.04` (state.pointer is updated by R3F's event pass; no listener needed). Delete lines 462-466 and the mouse ref.

[P3] hero-console.tsx:263-266 — dead cursor affordance on the scene label chip
  Evidence: the chip carries `data-cursor="rotate" data-cursor-label={t('placeholder')}` but also `pointer-events-none`, so pointerover can never target it (custom-cursor matches via closest() from e.target).
  Impact: the "rotate" cursor label promised over the console scene never activates; attribute pair is dead code.
  Recommended fix: move data-cursor/data-cursor-label onto the hit-testable container (#hero-console-scene div) or drop the attributes.
[P3] calculator.tsx:189 — aria-label + aria-labelledby conflict on the calculator section
  Evidence: `<section id="calculator" … aria-labelledby="calc-title" aria-label={t('kicker')}>` — when both are present aria-label wins.
  Impact: the landmark's accessible name becomes the short kicker instead of the full h2 title; the labelledby wiring is inert.
  Recommended fix: delete the `aria-label={t('kicker')}` attribute (keep labelledby).
[P3] contact/page.tsx:97 — section landmark labelled "channels" also contains the form + social proof
  Evidence: `<section className="bg-background py-20…" aria-labelledby="channels-title">` wraps the 2-col grid (channels list AND form column with its own h2 "form.title").
  Impact: SR users navigating by landmarks hear a "قنوات مباشرة" region that also holds the classic form — the region name under-describes half its content.
  Recommended fix: split into two sections (each labelled by its own h2) or relabel the outer one (e.g. a "تواصل" heading id).
[P3] contact-form.tsx:108-115 — prefill template is seeded once; stale on soft re-navigation
  Evidence: `useState(() => ({ …, message: buildTemplate(initialService, prefillIdea) }))` — buildTemplate runs only in the lazy initializer; props changes never re-seed.
  Impact: latent today (all prefill producers live on other routes), but a client-side locale switch on /contact?service=… keeps the seeded message in the OLD language, and any future same-route searchParam change would keep the stale template. User edits being preserved is correct; only the untouched-template case drifts.
  Recommended fix: if ever needed, re-seed in a [prefillService, prefillIdea, locale]-keyed effect guarded by "textarea still equals previous template" (same edit-protection as onToggleService).
[P3] calculator.tsx:518 + contact-form.tsx:227 — honeypot `-left-[9999px]` physical offset (documented RTL quirk)
  Evidence: `className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-[9999px] h-px w-px overflow-hidden"`.
  Impact: pre-existing + documented (UI-5 note: /contact body scrollWidth reads 11295 under RTL; not visually scrollable) — listed for completeness.
  Recommended fix: switch to the sr-only clip pattern or `position: fixed; inset-inline-start: -9999px` to kill the phantom scrollWidth.
[P3] deconstructed-card.tsx:163 — hardcoded Latin fallback metric
  Evidence: `const metricMain = metrics?.[0] ?? '+140%'`.
  Impact: unreachable while catalogs ship workSection.project1.metrics (verified present) — but if the key ever goes missing the showcase silently renders an English string on the AR site.
  Recommended fix: fall back to `''` or omit the metric line when absent.
[P3] about/page.tsx:124 — unreachable avatar-gradient fallback (dead code)
  Evidence: `AVATAR_GRADIENTS[i % AVATAR_GRADIENTS.length] ?? 'from-primary to-g-blue'` — modulo over a 4-entry array always yields a defined value.
  Impact: dead branch only; minor noise.
  Recommended fix: drop the `?? …` (or keep as a deliberate guard with a comment).
[P3] home compact interactive controls below 44px touch targets (documented scope decision)
  Evidence: hero-console preset chips `px-3 py-1.5 text-sm` (~33px), MiniAgent input/button h-9 (36px), simulator scenario toggles h-10 (40px), calculator option chips py-3 (~44px ok) but integration chips py-1.5 (~33px).
  Impact: sub-44px targets on the home conversion path (console chips are the hero's primary intent input). Batch 1 item 4 deliberately scoped the 44px rule to navbar; the remainder was left compact by design (4-I3 risk note 3).
  Recommended fix: if the rule is extended site-wide, bump chips to min-h-11 / py-2.5 in a single pass; otherwise record the scope decision as final.
POSITIVE VERIFICATIONS (no action): DamascusClock placeholder+'--:--:--' setState-in-effect pattern is hydration-safe; useSyncExternalStore server-snapshot pattern (reduced-motion, sound, global-error language) prevents hydration mismatches across all animated components; universal :lang(ar) letter-spacing reset neutralizes every tracking-* leak I found (kicker, calculator reference pill, simulator headings); dir="ltr" islands correct (clock, console/payload/log, orbit seal, URLs, phone values, formula bar); Next-16 Promise params/searchParams awaited everywhere; per-page generateMetadata via buildPageMetadata with canonical+hreflang+x-default and pinned OG cards; JSON-LD on home (no fake SearchAction); heading hierarchies clean on every route (sr-only h2 on /work fixes the old jump); [...rest] hard-404 + per-segment loading.tsx rationale verified; all internal nav links resolve to real routes; lazy wrappers genuinely code-split (dynamic import + IO-gated mount + matched placeholders); eslint 0/0 on all 33 files; R7 lessons correctly applied (data-bg-layer on all hero decorative absolutes, h1 flex-col, testimonial Parallax not a direct spotlight child).
VERDICT: 0 P0, 1 P1, 2 P2, 9 P3 — architecture is sound (hydration, a11y, i18n, SEO, perf discipline all verified strong); the one real debt is an incomplete amber→blue revert sweep concentrated in fallback/glow layers, plus two content/robustness gaps; everything else is polish.
ADDENDUM (L1-C, post-report self-correction): My [P2] console-scene WebGL-guard finding overclaimed the blast radius. Cross-checking @react-three/fiber v9.7 source (events-156d8d12.esm.js:15700-15743, 15928-15936) confirms L1-D's verification: Canvas root.configure() is async and un-awaited — a WebGLRenderer constructor failure rejects silently (unhandled promise rejection in console; children never mount), it does NOT propagate into React's render path, so [locale]/error.tsx never trips. Corrected impact: on browsers where getContext('webgl2'/'webgl') fails despite WebGLRenderingContext existing (blocklisted GPU/driver crash), the hero-console scene area is a ~280px empty dark box with a console error — a degraded-UX edge case, not a page crash. Corrected severity: P3 (probe context creation in the showSvg gate and/or add ContextLossGuard for parity with the other two canvases). Amended verdict: 0 P0, 1 P1, 2 P2, 10 P3 (the P2 slot reverts to the two content/consistency findings; the WebGL guard joins the P3 bucket).

Work Log:

- Deployment sequence: (a) row count via a /tmp bun script (kept OUT of the repo) = 0 rows (coordinator had cleaned earlier) → no deleteMany needed; (b) `bun run db:push` → success, Prisma client regenerated (v6.19.2); (c) smoke test `curl -X POST /api/leads` with a valid contact-form payload + matching Origin header (curl sends no Sec-Fetch-Site, so the CSRF layer passes header-less clients) → 201 {"reference":"c2dc9xjtdk"}; NO manual restart was needed — editing next.config.ts made Next 16 dev self-restart ("Found a change in next.config.ts. Restarting the server…"), which loaded the regenerated client (no "Unknown argument reference" ever appeared in dev.log); (d) /tmp verification script: row persisted WITH reference === the 201 value (service:'contact' sentinel + message stored); second smoke (calculator payload) → 201 {"reference":"cx1fuclsbz"} persisted with server-recomputed estimate 5800–9200 USD; (e) MANDATORY CLEANUP done — deleteMany({}) after verification → FINAL_COUNT: 0; (f) dev.log tail clean of prisma issues.
- Fix 2 (L1-B P3 — quota refund): rate-limit.ts gained `refundRateLimit(key, bucket)` — removes the NEWEST timestamp (module is synchronous on a single-threaded runtime; documented the bounded one-hit over-refund edge if a same-key request interleaves); route.ts catch-around-create calls `refundRateLimit(ip, 'strict')` before the 500 return (the lenient hit intentionally stays counted). Unit-tested in /tmp: 5 strict hits → 6th blocked → refund → allowed again; refund on unknown key is a safe no-op; lenient bucket untouched (11/11 PASS).
- Fix 3 (L1-A P3 — inline size guard): rate-limit.ts sweep() split into sweepExpired(now) + evictOverCap() (MAX_KEYS=5_000 / EVICT_BATCH=1_000 as named constants, behavior preserved); rateLimit() now runs the cleanup INLINE when a brand-new key would be inserted while the Map is at/over the cap (existing===undefined && hits.size>=MAX_KEYS) — Map stays bounded between the 60s sweeps (worst overshoot: 1 key). Behavioral test: 6000-key flood → no throw, all allowed.
- Fix 4 (L1-A/L1-B P3): n8n-webhook.ts deliver() dropped the never-read `secret` parameter (transport-only doc comment added — signing stays in attemptDelivery); call site now `deliver(config.url, body, headers)`. Grep: no other callers exist.
- Fix 5 (L1-A P3): robots.ts Disallow ['/api','/admin'] → ['/api'] + comment explaining why /admin stays out (nonexistent path = free recon hint). Live robots.txt verified: /admin gone.
- Fix 6 (L1-A P3): home-json-ld.tsx — JSON.stringify(...).replace(/</g,'\\u003c') on the dangerouslySetInnerHTML payload; proof via bun: {'x':'</script><img src=x>'} → {"x":"\u003c/script>\u003c<img src=x>"} (zero raw '<', still valid JSON). Current build-time data contains no '<' so today's output is byte-identical (live JSON-LD verified on the 200 homepage).
- Fix 7 (L1-A P2): next.config.ts securityHeaders += Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin and Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin (comment: no-ops for the app's own functionality — no popups, same-origin assets). Live-verified present on curl -I /.
- Fix 8 (L1-A P2 — TRUST_PROXY posture): Dockerfile runtime ENV block += TRUST_PROXY=true (+rationale comment; header run-comment updated); README docker run example += -e TRUST_PROXY=true plus an Arabic explanation of the single-global-bucket lockout it prevents.

Work Log:
